{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_014hH6rPf6prVGhvgS3qrhrq","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01GrSywjgpbFDS5FmSJhVkR9","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westb-48145","command":"source /root/miniconda3/bin/activate && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python -c \"\nfrom fla.models.retnet import RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM\nfrom transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\nimport torch\nAutoConfig.register('retnet', RetNetConfig)\nAutoModelForCausalLM.register(RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM)\n\npath = '/root/autodl-tmp/models_1.3b/1.3B-100B-RetNet-pure'\nconfig = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(path)\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(path, config=config, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16)\nmodel.tie_weights()\nmodel = model.to('cuda:0').eval()\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(path)\n\ninput_ids = tokenizer('The capital of France is', return_tensors='pt').input_ids.to('cuda:0')\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    outputs = model(input_ids)\n    logits = outputs.logits\n    probs = torch.softmax(logits[0, -1], dim=-1)\n    top5 = torch.topk(probs, 5)\n    for i in range(5):\n        tok = tokenizer.decode(top5.indices[i])\n        print(f'top{i+1}: {tok!r} ({top5.values[i].item():.4f})')\nprint('RetNet loaded OK')\n\" 2>&1 | grep -E \"top|OK|Error|Traceback\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":540,"cache_read_input_tokens":149476,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":540},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"2da0f64f-0859-482e-a8e7-df9a777466f3","uuid":"c4717177-e97e-4555-af4d-d3fb4018752d"}

 ```python\n     #!/usr/bin/env python3\n     import argparse\n     import json\n     import os\n     import torch\n     from transformers import AutoConfig, AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\n     from fla.models.gated_deltanet import GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM\n     from fla.models.retnet import RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM\n     from fla.models.transformer import TransformerConfig, TransformerForCausalLM\n\n     AutoConfig.register(\"gated_deltanet\", GatedDeltaNetConfig)\n     AutoModelForCausalLM.register(GatedDeltaNetConfig, GatedDeltaNetForCausalLM)\n     AutoConfig.register(\"retnet\", RetNetConfig)\n     AutoModelForCausalLM.register(RetNetConfig, RetNetForCausalLM)\n     AutoConfig.register(\"transformer\", TransformerConfig)\n     AutoModelForCausalLM.register(TransformerConfig, TransformerForCausalLM)\n\n     def main():\n         parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n         parser.add_argument(\"--model_path\", required=True)\n         parser.add_argument(\"--tasks\", default=\"lambada_openai,hellaswag,winogrande,piqa,arc_easy,arc_challenge,mmlu\")\n         parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/entrainment_gated_ft/lm_eval_clean\")\n         parser.add_argument(\"--batch_size\", default=\"auto\")\n         parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:0\")\n         args = parser.parse_args()\n\n         tasks_list = [t.strip() for t in args.tasks.split(\",\")]\n         model_name = os.path.basename(args.model_path.rstrip(\"/\"))\n\n         config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(args.model_path)\n         print(f\"model_type={config.model_type}, tie_word_embeddings={getattr(config, 'tie_word_embeddings', None)}\")\n\n         if getattr(config, \"use_beta\", False):\n             config.allow_neg_eigval = True\n             print(f\"[fix] allow_neg_eigval=True\")\n\n         model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n             args.model_path, config=config, torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16\n         )\n\n         if getattr(config, \"tie_word_embeddings\", False):\n             model.tie_weights()\n             print(f\"[fix] tie_weights()\")\n\n         model = model.to(args.device).eval()\n         tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(args.model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n\n         from lm_eval.models.huggingface import HFLM\n         import lm_eval\n\n         batch_size = int(args.batch_size) if args.batch_size.isdigit() else args.batch_size\n         lm_obj = HFLM(pretrained=model, tokenizer=tokenizer, batch_size=batch_size)\n\n         print(f\"Running tasks: {tasks_list}\")\n         results = lm_eval.simple_evaluate(model=lm_obj, tasks=tasks_list)\n\n         if hasattr(results, \"__dict__\"):\n             raw = {k: v for k, v in results.__dict__.items() if not k.startswith(\"_\")}\n         elif isinstance(results, dict):\n             raw = output_file = os.path.join(args.output_dir, f\"{model_name}.json\")\n         with open(output_file, \"w\") as f:\n             json.dump(raw, f, indent=2, default=str)\n\n         print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n         print(f\"Model: {model_name}\")\n         print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n         if isinstance(results_dict, dict):\n             for task in sorted(results_dict.keys()):\n                 metrics = results_dict[task]\n                 if isinstance(metrics, dict):\n                     parts = []\n                     for k in sorted(metrics.keys()):\n                         v = metrics[k]\n                         if isinstance(v, float) and \"stderr\" not in k:\n                             parts.append(f\"{k}={v:.4f}\")\n                     if parts:\n This corrupted GDN pure (tie_word_embeddings=false) giving acc=0.0, ppl=432M. Fix: removed the injected code (lines 389-396). After fix: GDN pure acc=0.5428, ppl=8.26.\n   \n   - **RetNet `_tied_weights_keys` incompatibility**: transformers 5.8.1's `get_expanded_tied_weights_keys()` expects `_tied_weights_keys` as dict but fla 0.5.0 provides list. Used `nohup` and `screen` for long-running processes.\n\n5.
